# No Easy Way Out

Journal: `ep1/quests/minor_quest/mq306_dumpster` · `MinorQuest` · hash `3914226168`.

Journal premise: Look, V. This Aaron choom is a big-handed bastard who knows how to handle himself in hot water. Sure, we gotta help each other out and all that, but watch your back. You’re about to walk into the lion’s den. I know you’re all about helping lost souls and walking elderly ladies across the street, but please, PLEASE don’t get killed assisting some lesser Dogtown boxer.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 133 exact journal-linked nodes in 12 resources; 13 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 12 content candidate, 1 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1429 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 209 distinct direct resource identities; 60 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
